### 2026-05-29 23:47:40  #Trading Economics #Markets #Germany #Stock Market #Importance 0

DAX Ends May Month Higher — The DAX 40 finished 0.1% higher at 25,113 on Friday and ended May up 3.4%, supported by rising hopes of a near-term US–Iran agreement. In the latest developments, US President Trump said he was meeting in the White House Situation Room to make a final determination on a deal with Iran. Meanwhile, oil prices fell to more than one-month lows, providing some relief to inflationary pressures. Flash estimates showed the EU-harmonised annual inflation rate in Germany eased to 2.7% in May from 2.9% in April, slightly below forecasts of 2.8%. SAP (+2.8%) and Zalando (+3.0%) were among the top performers, while Bayer (-3.9%), Siemens Energy (-2.8%), and Beiersdorf (-2.8%) underperformed.

### 2026-05-29 23:47:10  #Trading Economics #Markets #United Kingdom #Stock Market #Importance 1

FTSE 100 Edges Up, Advances for 2nd Month — The FTSE 100 edged slightly higher on Friday and rose for a second month, though the index is still around 4.4% below levels seen before the war began. Sentiment was supported by positive developments in the Middle East after reports that the US and Iran agreed to extend their ceasefire by 60 days, pending approval from President Donald Trump. Oil prices moved lower on expectations that the truce could hold. AstraZeneca gained around 0.3% after its blockbuster drug Imfinzi received US approval for the treatment of early bladder cancer. Defence stocks also advanced, with BAE Systems up 0.5% and Rolls Royce gaining 1.9%. Among banks, HSBC added 1.2%, Lloyds rose 0.5% and Barclays gained 1.9%. On the other hand, Shell fell more than 1%, as well as Unilever. BAT dropped more than 2.5% and GSK fell over 1%.

### 2026-05-29 23:32:35  #公司 #國際

【瑞銀收購瑞信後持續瘦身 再度裁員數百人】瑞銀集團在歐洲、中東及非洲地區裁減數百個崗位，這是該行三年前收購瑞信以來實施的最新一輪相關裁員。知情人士透露，此次裁員主要波及後勤支援部門的員工，但也影響到一些對接客戶的銀行家。知情人士表示，為了減輕裁員的影響，瑞銀為部分被裁員工提供了公司內部的新職位。瑞銀一位發言人表示，無論是在瑞士本土還是在全球範圍內，該行都將儘可能把收購導致的裁員人數控制在“儘可能低”的水平。發言人還稱，為減少冗餘，瑞銀致力於將目前由第三方供應商承擔的崗位轉移到內部。最新資料顯示，自完成收購瑞信的交易以來，瑞銀的員工總數已減少了約17500人。彭博此前曾報道稱，在交易完成後的幾個月裡，該行曾計劃最終裁員約35000人。

### 2026-05-29 23:25:50  #MKT News #Market Regions #Europe & UK #EURUSD #Impact bullish #DAX

【EU to unfreeze €16.4bln for Hungary】The European Commission will unfreeze €16.4bln in EU funds for Hungary, von der Leyen said on the 29th. The package includes €10.0bln from NextGenerationEU, €4.2bln tied to cohesion-fund conditions and €2.2bln released after progress on university autonomy. Von der Leyen said the commission and Hungary reached agreement on rule-of-law and anti-corruption steps; Hungary has agreed to join the European Public Prosecutor’s Office, strengthen anti-corruption bodies and amend public procurement law. Prime Minister Magyar said the funds equal about 13% of Hungary’s annual budget and will be used for SMEs, transport, healthcare, education, energy infrastructure and digital technology.

### 2026-05-29 23:25:31  #國際

【歐盟將解凍匈牙利164億歐元歐盟資金】歐盟委員會主席馮德萊恩29日說，歐盟將解凍屬於匈牙利的164億歐元歐盟資金。馮德萊恩在同匈牙利總理毛焦爾·彼得會談後舉行的聯合記者會上說，這164億歐元包括“下一代歐盟”計劃的100億歐元、與凝聚基金條件相關的42億歐元，以及因匈牙利在高校自主權方面取得進展而釋放的22億歐元。馮德萊恩說，匈牙利新政府和歐盟委員會就應對腐敗和法治方面的問題達成一致。匈牙利已決定加入歐洲檢察官辦公室，以保障歐盟資金安全，同時同意加強廉政機構權力、修訂公共採購法等。毛焦爾表示，這筆資金相當於匈牙利政府年度預算的約13%，將被用於支援中小企業、交通、醫療、教育、能源基礎設施和數字技術發展等領域。（新華社）

### 2026-05-29 23:22:07  #Trading Economics #Economy #Colombia #Unemployment Rate #Importance 1

Colombia Jobless Rate Unchanged at 8.8% — Colombia’s national unemployment rate stood at 8.8% in April 2026, unchanged from the previous month and in line with forecasts. The unemployment rate for women was 10.9%, compared with 7.1% for men, resulting in a gender gap of 3.8 percentage points. In the country’s 13 main cities and metropolitan areas, the unemployment rate also stood at 8.8%. The number of unemployed people nationwide increased by 67,000 from a year earlier, a 3% rise, mainly driven by smaller urban areas and rural centers, where unemployment rose 8%. During the February-April 2026 rolling quarter, the national unemployment rate averaged 8.9%.

### 2026-05-29 23:13:19  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Government Budget Value #Importance 1

Canada’s Budget Deficit Widens in March — Canada’s federal budget deficit expanded to CAD 29.7 billion in March 2026, up from CAD 23.9 billion in the same month a year earlier. The budgetary deficit before net actuarial losses, excluding the impact of amortizing pension and employee benefit plan revaluations, narrowed to CAD 20.6 billion from CAD 23.5 billion in March 2025. Net actuarial losses surged by 240.7%, primarily due to the accelerated amortization of losses from amendments to employee future benefit plans in 2025-26. Meanwhile, program expenses excluding actuarial losses fell by 1.9%, driven by lower direct costs following savings from benefit plan amendments announced in Budget 2025. Revenues increased by 1.1%, supported by higher personal and corporate income tax receipts, other revenues, and customs import duties tied to countermeasures against US tariffs.

【德商銀行：在液化天然氣供應干擾的情況下，歐洲天然氣價格料將保持高位】預計到今年年底，歐洲天然氣價格將保持在遠高於戰前的水平，原因是在透過霍爾木茲海峽的液化天然氣供應持續受到干擾的情況下，該地區補充天然氣儲備設施的進度預計將出現延遲。德國商業銀行的Norman Liebke表示，歐洲目前正處於冬季來臨前關鍵的夏季儲備註入期，但液化天然氣供應量減少（尤其是來自卡達的供應）使得重建庫存變得更加困難。該機構表示，較低的庫存將使歐洲更容易受到供應衝擊和冬季需求激增的影響，從而使價格保持在每兆瓦時50歐元左右的高位。與此同時，如果與厄爾尼諾現象相關的炎熱天氣在今夏提振亞洲各地的液化天然氣需求，加劇與歐洲對可用船貨的競爭，則仍存在額外的上行風險。

【法國連續七年成為外國直接投資在歐洲的首選地】國際諮詢企業安永公司近日釋出的《2026年歐洲投資吸引力調查報告》顯示，法國2025年連續第七年成為外國直接投資在歐洲的首選目的地。 報告說，法國2025年共吸引852個外國投資專案，領先於英國的730個和德國的548個。其中，工業投資專案354個，佔比42%，顯示法國繼續保持歐洲製造業投資領先地位。軟體及資訊科技服務、國防和低碳能源等行業成為支撐法國投資吸引力的重要領域。 不過，歐洲整體投資吸引力仍承壓。報告指出，2025年歐洲吸引的外國直接投資專案數量下降7%，成為全球國際投資降幅最大的地區之一。 另據法國商務投資署此前釋出的報告，2025年中國進一步鞏固了作為法國重要經濟合作伙伴的地位，在對法投資來源國中位居前列，在亞洲國家中保持首位。從投資專案數量及創造或維持就業崗位數量看，中國投資均表現突出。(新華社)

【戴爾股價創兩年來最大漲幅 AI伺服器需求提振業績展望】戴爾科技股價週五創下兩年來的最大漲幅，此前這家硬體製造商給出的年度銷售展望遠超分析師預期，得益於人工智慧(AI)伺服器需求推動。這家總部位於德克薩斯州的公司週四在宣告中表示，截至2027年1月的財年營收將在約1,670億美元，其中600億美元來自AI伺服器銷售。這高於此前約1,400億美元的營收展望，也超過彭博彙編資料顯示的分析師平均預期1,421億美元。營運長Jeff Clarke在宣告中表示，在截至5月1日的季度，該公司獲得244億美元AI訂單，實現161億美元AI伺服器銷售額。“AI機遇未見放緩跡象。”紐約股市開盤後，該股一度大漲35%至428.68美元，創下自2024年3月以來的最大盤中漲幅，並重新整理歷史高點。戴爾的伺服器業務今年被視為AI贏家，推動該股截至週四收盤累計上漲逾150%。

【研究顯示算力正在滲透賦能更多行業】記者在此間召開的2026世界智慧產業博覽會上瞭解到，相關研究機構釋出的《2026全球AI算力發展研究報告》指出，算力正從科學智慧、具身智慧等前沿領域向工業、能源、交通等行業全面滲透。5月29日，在智博會期間舉行的“人工智慧創新與太空數智設施發展交流會”上，中國智慧計算產業聯盟、國家超級計算天津中心、天津市人工智慧學會、深圳市人工智慧行業協會等聯合釋出《2026全球AI算力發展研究報告》，並預測全球算力產業十大趨勢。報告認為全球算力產業正邁入“智算驅動、體系重構”的全新發展階段，並預測未來超節點與高速互聯有效提升算力效能，將成為全球構建新型算力基礎設施的重要路徑；隨著多智慧體框架出現，AI應用從互動智慧邁向執行智慧，催生新的推理算力需求。報告認為，AI使科學發現方式從傳統的經驗驅動逐步轉向模型驅動，深刻賦能合成生物學、地球與環境科學等發展；同時，算力深度影響具身智慧、自動駕駛、工業智慧和交通智慧等多行業。（新華社）

【聯合國人權高專呼籲加強兒童網路保護】聯合國人權事務高階專員沃爾克·圖爾克29日呼籲各國政府和科技公司採取更有力措施，為兒童打造更加安全的網路環境，並強調要進行有效的監管、監督和問責。圖爾克在一份宣告中說：“數字世界將兒童與學習、社群和創造力連線起來，同時也使他們的安全、隱私和福祉面臨切實風險。”他指出，兒童面臨的網路危害並非不可避免，它們源於那些破壞安全性的設計選擇和商業行為，例如無限滾動、內容自動播放以及應用程式的持續通知等成癮性設計功能。“加強對兒童在網路環境中的保護是一項緊迫的優先事項，我們要確保這項工作不僅要做成，而且要做對。”圖爾克說。針對部分國家出臺法規限制兒童和青少年群體使用社交媒體的做法，圖爾克表示，相關措施並不能一勞永逸地解決問題。為了有效保護兒童，政府和企業需要採取更廣泛行動，確保網路平臺在設計之初就將安全性納入考量，實施資料保護，對造成傷害的人進行問責，並使兒童的權利和需求始終得到充分尊重。他還指出，無論採取何種規定，都應避免無意中造成進一步傷害。例如，年齡驗證操作不當不僅可能達不到預期目的，還會危及兒童和成年人的隱私等。聯合國人權事務高階專員辦事處當天還發布“正確保障兒童網路安全”的十項指導原則，相關建議措施包括在年齡驗證流程中設定保障機制，強制進行兒童權利影響評估等。（新華社）

Sri Lanka Trade Deficit Hits Record High — Sri Lanka’s trade deficit widened to $1.38 billion in April 2026, the largest gap since records began in 2002 and up 27% from a year earlier. Goods imports surged 45.7% year-on-year to $2.46 billion, driven by a 149.9% jump in fuel imports to $886 million amid soaring global oil prices linked to the Middle East conflict and higher import volumes. Motor vehicle imports also totaled $208 million. Meanwhile, exports increased 10.9% to $1.07 billion. In the first four months of 2026, the trade deficit widened to $3.7 billion from $2.3 billion in the same period of 2025.

【受伊朗戰事波及的大型油輪中，已有四分之一成功駛出海峽】在伊朗戰事爆發之初滯留在波斯灣內的非伊朗籍大型油輪中，已有約有四分之一的油輪透過緩慢且隱秘的方式逐漸脫困，成功駛離該海域。據媒體彙編的航運資料顯示，在2月28日衝突爆發、霍爾木茲海峽實際上處於封閉狀態後，共有109艘大型船隻（即載運能力在70萬桶及以上的船隻）受困；如今，其中已有29艘成功穿越了這一戰略咽喉要道。儘管這些已流出的貨物僅佔海灣內仍滯留的原油及成品油總量的極小一部分，但在全球市場庫存緩衝正以創紀錄的速度縮減的大背景下，這些貨物一經流出便迅速被市場搶購一空。此外，鑑於許多船隻為規避風險而關閉了位置廣播裝置，實際成功撤離的船隻數量很可能還要高於上述統計數字。

【About a quarter of large tankers stranded by Iran conflict have exited the Strait of Hormuz】Bloomberg shipping data show that of 109 large tankers (>=700,000 barrels) trapped in the Persian Gulf after the Feb. 28 outbreak and the effective closure of the Strait of Hormuz, 29 — roughly one quarter — have since made slow, covert departures and transited the strait. Those cargoes account for only a small portion of oil and refined products still held in the Gulf, but were rapidly bought up as global inventory buffers are shrinking at a record pace. Many vessels have been operating with AIS transponders off, so the true number of successful evacuations is likely higher.

【美銀：全球股票基金九周以來首次出現資金流出】國銀行表示，全球股市九周以來首次出現資金流出，現金和債券資產吸引資金流入。美銀援引EPFR Global的資料指出，截至5月27日的一週，貨幣市場基金吸引資金219億美元，債券吸引了236億美元。與此同時，股票市場遭遇70億美元的資金流出，加密貨幣流出12億美元，黃金流出10億美元。值得關注的資金流向包括：日本股市遭遇2025年5月以來最大規模資金流出，流出額達82億美元。由Michael Hartnett領導的美銀策略師團隊指出，儘管標普500指數創下歷史新高，但僅有21只成分股（佔該基準指數的4%）創下新高。相比之下，在2000年3月網際網路泡沫破裂前，僅有20只成分股創下新高。該團隊表示，參考1929年以來歷次泡沫破裂後的投資路線圖，做多債券往往是一個選項，因為在市場大頂部出現後的6個月內，10年期國債收益率通常會下降約50個基點。此外，還可以做多防禦性資產以及/或者在泡沫最後幾個月裡表現極為落後的股票板塊/風格。

Dollar Near 2-Week Lows, Still on Track for Monthly Gain — The dollar index was little changed at 99 on Friday, close to two-week lows, but was on track to end the May month about 1% higher, as investors continued to monitor developments in the Middle East. Signs have emerged that the US and Iran may be closer than ever to reaching an agreement, with recent reports indicating that both countries have reached a preliminary understanding to extend a ceasefire by 60 days and begin discussions over the future of Tehran’s nuclear program, although President Trump has yet to formally endorse the terms. Oil prices have declined, helping to ease inflationary pressures. Data released on Thursday showed that both headline and core PCE monthly inflation came in below expectations although annual readings remained well above the Fed’s target at 3.8% and 3.3%. Investors currently expect the Fed to keep the federal funds rate unchanged through year-end, though markets still assign roughly a 46% probability to a rate hike in December.

【尼泊爾部分地區因強降雨引發災害，中使館提醒注意雨季安全】5月29日，據“中國駐尼泊爾大使館”公眾號訊息，近期，尼泊爾部分地區因強降雨引發災害，造成嚴重人員傷亡和財產損失。尼泊爾即將進入雨季（一般自6月持續至9月），其間易發生洪澇、山體滑坡、道路塌方等地質災害，腹瀉、傷寒等介水傳染病和登革熱、瘧疾病例也呈高發態勢。中國駐尼泊爾大使館提醒在尼泊爾中國公民和機構:一、密切關注天氣狀況，及時檢視氣象預報和各類預警資訊，儘可能避免或減少在極端天氣出行。二、注意出行安全，選擇安全可靠的交通工具和路線，嚴格遵守當地交通規則，關注實時路況，儘量避開山區河谷、江河沿岸、低窪易澇等地質災害高發區域。三、切勿主動涉險，非必要不進山、不涉水、不前往偏僻、通訊設施不完善區域。確需前往的，請嚴格遵守當地政府部門預警提示。四、瞭解必要自救和防災防疫常識，結合自身風險應對能力做好預案。儘量規避前往登革熱病例高發區域，日常做好居所防蚊、滅蚊和環境清潔工作。加強健康監測。如出現高熱、劇烈頭痛、關節肌肉疼痛、皮疹等不適症狀，應儘快就醫。五、時刻繃緊安全生產這根弦。提高風險防範意識，加強監測預報預警，深入排查整治各類風險隱患。切實加強安全生產監督管理，鞏固施工作業安全防護措施，堅決防範避免重特大安全事故。提前做好防災物資準備，提高救災應對能力，切實保障人身與財產安全。六、如突遇災害，請全力保持聯絡通暢，遵從當地官方指引及時轉移避險，注意防範次生災害。七、應急求助電話尼泊爾報警電話：100尼泊爾急救電話：102尼泊爾旅遊警察電話：1144或01-4247041尼泊爾直升機救援電話：+977-9801050000外交部全球領事保護與服務應急熱線（24小時）：+86-10-12308、+86-10-65612308中國駐尼泊爾大使館領事保護與協助電話：+977-1-4531511

Signs have emerged that the US and Iran may be closer than ever to reaching an agreement, with recent reports indicating that both countries have reached a preliminary understanding to extend a ceasefire by 60 days and begin discussions over the future of Tehran’s nuclear program, although President Trump has yet to formally endorse the terms. Oil prices have declined, helping to ease inflationary pressures. Data released on Thursday showed that both headline and core PCE monthly inflation came in below expectations although annual readings remained well above the Fed’s target at 3.8% and 3.3%. Investors currently expect the Fed to keep the federal funds rate unchanged through year-end, though markets still assign roughly a 46% probability to a rate hike in December.

【US media: US-Iran draft would create $300 bln fund, halt fighting in Lebanon】US media (i24News citing the New York Times) report a U.S.-Iran draft understanding under negotiation that would temporarily ease hostilities and pair parallel political and economic commitments to pave the way for a long-term deal. 【Fed Governor Bowman: Too early to judge Iran war's impact on inflation; temporary 'look-through' on price shocks appropriate】Fed Governor Bowman said it is too early to assess the Iran war's impact on inflation and policymakers should adopt a "temporary look-through" stance toward short-lived price shocks. She backed keeping language in last month's post-meeting statement that left open the possibility of further rate cuts, a position that contrasts with a growing number of colleagues who want the Fed to signal the next move could be either a hike or a cut. Bowman said she wants clearer evidence on the economic impact and persistence of the Middle East conflict before revising the expected policy path and that temporarily overlooking energy-driven upticks in inflation is appropriate so long as the Fed preserves its credibility on the inflation target.

### 2026-05-29 21:13:36  #Trading Economics #Economy #Chile #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Chile Industrial Output Falls in April — Chile’s industrial production fell 4.7% year-on-year in April 2026, following a 3.4% contraction in the previous month. The decline was mainly driven by mining activity, as the mining production index dropped 9.0% due to a 12.6% contraction in metallic mining. Copper extraction and processing fell 13.8%, reflecting a high comparison base and lower ore grades at major producers. Manufacturing output also weakened, declining 2.5%, largely due to a 7.7% drop in food production. In contrast, utilities output rose 0.9%, supported by a 2.3% increase in electricity generation. On a monthly basis, industrial production edged down 0.2%.

### 2026-05-29 21:12:38  #Trading Economics #Economy #Chile #Retail Sales YoY #Importance 1

Chile Retail Sales Rise 4.1% YoY in April — Retail sales in Chile increased 4.1% year-on-year in April 2026, slowing from a downwardly revised 4.5% rise in March. The most significant contributions came from sales of miscellaneous consumer goods (6.8%), new light motor vehicles (15.6%), and motor vehicle spare parts, tires, and accessories (10%). Compared to the previous month, retail sales declined 0.5%, the biggest drop since July last year, following an upwardly revised 0.3% rise in March.

The US and Iran agreed on Thursday to extend their ceasefire and lift restrictions on shipping through the Strait of Hormuz, although the deal still requires approval from US President Donald Trump. Oil prices retreated, pushing bond yields lower as energy-driven inflation concerns eased, supporting banks and the broader index amid reduced worries over higher borrowing costs. Meanwhile, Canadian GDP stalled in the first quarter of 2026, matching the 0.2% contraction recorded in the final quarter of 2025. ### 2026-05-29 20:58:12  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Africa #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

South Africa Trade Surplus Shrinks in April — South Africa's trade surplus narrowed to ZAR 15.2 billion in April 2026 from a downwardly revised ZAR 30.2 billion in the previous month, as a sharp rise in imports outpaced export growth. Imports soared 11.8% to ZAR 175 billion, largely due to a 76% jump in purchases of mineral products, alongside a 5% increase in machinery & electronics. Conversely, declines were seen for precious metals & stones (-21%); textiles (-17%) and original equipment components (-10%). Meanwhile, exports rose 1.8% to ZAR 191 billion, driven by a 30% surge in precious metals and stones, which offset weaker shipments of vegetable products (-15%), chemicals (-14%), mineral products (-5%) and transport equipment (-5%).

### 2026-05-29 20:57:28  #MKT News #Market Regions #Greater China #Hang Seng Index

【Hong Kong insurance regulator says it will continue to crack down on improper cross‑border sales, maintains contact with Mainland China ministries】Hong Kong’s insurance regulator told investors it remains in close contact with Mainland China ministries on regulatory issues including illegal cross‑border sales. Under current rules, life insurance sales must be completed in Hong Kong and licensed Hong Kong insurance intermediaries are prohibited from soliciting business in the mainland. Principals (insurers, brokers or agencies) must implement effective internal controls to ensure intermediaries act lawfully. Since 2016, additional supervisory requirements obligate mainland purchasers to sign an Important Information Declaration, require all sales and policy documents to be signed in Hong Kong, and require insurers to collect supporting evidence (eg, entry records) proving the customer was physically in Hong Kong at application.

### 2026-05-29 20:54:58  #公司 #市場

【香港保監局回應跨境投保：繼續打擊市場不當行為 和內地相關部委一直保持溝通】近日，中國證監會針對非法跨境證券期貨基金業務活動的整治行動受到廣泛關注，也引發部分中國內地投保人對購買香港保險合規性的擔憂。市場上也不乏關於“跨境保險會否成為下一個更嚴監管及整治領域”的討論。針對內地居民購買港險合規性問題，香港保險業監管局於5月29日獨家回覆記者稱，香港保監局和內地相關部委一直保持密切聯絡，就包括違規跨境銷售在內的監管議題進行溝通。在現行法規下，香港人壽保險的整個銷售流程必須在香港境內進行，而香港的持牌保險中介人亦不得在內地招攬保險業務。主事人（即保險公司、保險經紀公司或保險代理機構）有責任制定和實施有效的內部管控程式，以確保中介人合法合規地進行受規管活動。上述回覆提到，就內地人士來港投保事宜，香港保監局自2016年起已實施額外監管要求，特別規定內地人士來港投保時須另行簽署一份“重要資料宣告”，以確保保單的整個銷售過程必須在香港境內進行，且所有投保檔案亦必須在香港境內簽署。除此之外，保險公司亦須收集相關佐證檔案（例如入境檔案記錄等），以證明該內地客戶在投保時確實身處香港。 (第一財經)

【標普500指數出現“雙西格瑪”級別漲幅 未來恐難賺“快錢”】標普500指數已重返歷史新高，但毫無疑問，華爾街過去兩個月那波令人驚歎的凌厲漲勢已顯著放緩。該基準指數在短短41個交易日內，便從3月份的低點飆升了超過19%。DataTrek Research的分析師將此輪反彈形容為已逼近“雙西格瑪”（two sigma）級別的漲幅，這意味著其漲幅已偏離任意100天平均回報率的兩個標準差。這可能預示著眼下的“快錢”已經賺完；投資者應做好準備，迎接美國夏季期間市場更為緩慢的震盪上行——甚至可能是區間震盪的行情。DataTrek公司的分析師Jessica Rabe在一份報告中寫道：“歷史經驗表明，標普指數可能將在此處進入盤整期，其上漲步伐預計將大幅放緩。”

### 2026-05-29 20:53:35  #其他

【《網際網路資訊內容多渠道分發服務管理規定》答記者問】四、問：《規定》對設立執行網際網路資訊內容多渠道分發服務提出了哪些要求？答：《規定》對設立執行網際網路資訊內容多渠道分發服務提出了明確要求。一是規定網際網路資訊內容多渠道分發服務機構應當依法辦理經營主體登記，並依法取得相應行政許可；應當設立資訊內容管理負責人，配備相應的內容管理團隊並制定相關規則。二是明確平臺資訊服務提供者應當及時與透過本平臺提供網際網路資訊內容多渠道分發服務的機構簽訂入駐協議，依法依約進行核驗，並將入駐情況報所在地省級網信部門備案。三是要求平臺資訊服務提供者根據法律、行政法規和國家有關規定，在平臺規則中明確網際網路資訊內容多渠道分發服務規範，建立分類分級管理制度，並採取相應管理措施防範資訊保安風險。四是規定平臺資訊服務提供者應當要求機構註冊後臺管理賬號，加強對簽約網際網路使用者公眾賬號的管理，以顯著方式展示簽約網際網路使用者公眾賬號所屬網際網路資訊內容多渠道分發服務機構名稱，並依法依約加強對機構服務活動的規範。五是要求網際網路資訊內容多渠道分發服務機構與網際網路使用者公眾賬號生產運營者簽訂服務協議時依法核實其身份；為網際網路使用者公眾賬號生產運營者提供製作、複製、釋出、傳播網際網路資訊內容相關服務的，還應當在服務協議中明確網際網路資訊內容管理責任，並依法依約履行資訊保安管理義務。六是明確平臺資訊服務提供者應當建立相關投訴、舉報通道並及時受理、處理。（中國網信辦）

【分析：瑞典經濟橫向盤整，但未見任何戰爭影響跡象】DNB Carnegie的瑞典首席經濟學家Ulf Andersson寫道，今年第一季度瑞典經濟橫向盤整，不過沒有明顯跡象表明受到了中東衝突的影響。他說，2026年的謹慎開區域性分反映了2025年第四季度較高的國防相關支出。4月份的零售額也已公佈，顯示銷售額環比持平，同比增長4.7%。週四公佈的5月份經濟趨勢指標顯示，景氣度基本保持中性。“到目前為止，本週的資料幾乎沒有提供任何證據表明中東衝突產生了任何實質性影響。”DNB Carnegie預計瑞典央行將在6月份維持政策利率不變，隨後在11月份加息。

Canada GDP Set to Rebound in April — The Canadian economy likely expanded 0.4% month-over-month in April 2026, the most since January 2025, recovering from a 0.1% drop in March, according to preliminary estimates. In March, the GDP shrank for the first time in five months, mainly driven by a 0.8% contraction in goods-producing industries. Oil and gas extraction decreased 2% and oil and gas extraction (except oil sands) was down 2.3%, the largest drop in over a year, as lower crude petroleum extraction in the Western provinces and Atlantic Canada contributed to the decline. Lower natural gas extraction further contributed. In addition, the construction sector was down 0.6% and retail trade contracted 0.6% while wholesale trade rose 1.8%, to mark the largest back-to-back monthly growth rates in over four years.

### 2026-05-29 20:39:07  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #GDP Growth Rate #Importance 2

Canada GDP Unexpectedly Stalls — The Canadian GDP stalled in the first quarter of 2026, holding the 0.2% contraction from the last quarter of 2025, and contrasting with market expectations that it would expand by 0.3%. Consumption expenditure inched higher by 0.2%, slowing from the 0.7% jump in the previous period, with household consumption rising by 0.4% to offset a 0.2% drop in government expenditure. In turn, gross fixed capital formation contracted by 1.1%, the first drop in one year. In turn, the GDP was pressured significantly by a 2.9% increase in imports, particularly for gold and scrap metal. Meanwhile, exports fell by 0.1% despite the surging oil and gas prices during March due to the outbreak of war in the Middle East. Much of the gold imported, in addition to manufactured goods were added to inventory, preventing a second contraction in the Canadian GDP. In annualized terms, Canada's GDP inched lower by 0.1%.

### 2026-05-29 20:39:01  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Wholesale Inventories #Importance 1

US Wholesale Inventory Growth Slows in April — US wholesale inventories advanced by 0.5% month-over-month to $938.6 billion in April 2026, following an upwardly revised 1.5% increase in March and compared with market forecasts for a 0.6% rise. This marked the third consecutive monthly increase in wholesale inventories, but the pace moderated from March, which saw the strongest growth since August 2022. Stocks of durable goods rose by 0.9%, after a 0.5% increase in March, while those of nondurables decreased by 0.2%, after a 3.2% advance in the prior month. On a yearly basis, wholesale inventories rose by 3.4%.

### 2026-05-29 20:36:57  #國際

【分析師：日本匯市干預規模驚人，但選錯時機恐讓巨資打水漂】分析師Justin Low表示，日本4月至5月期間匯市干預規模為11.7萬億日元，這是一個相當驚人的數字，這一次的規模超過了2022和2024年裡任何單一時期的干預規模。這種支出金額無疑意味著他們在最近的干預行動中並未手軟。問題在於，時機選擇相當不利，這使得市場能夠更猛烈地進行反向推升。首先，自美伊衝突開始以來，所有基本面因素都對日元極為不利。其次，干預的時機並非最佳，因為他們選擇在5月初日本市場假期期間進行。在流動性較低的時期不採取行動，聽起來可能違反直覺，但其中存在一定的微妙之處。干預的重點與其說是資金，不如說是訊號傳遞。需要有足夠多的市場參與者接收到這個訊號並將其放大，從而形成“不該與日本財務省對著幹”的共識。否則，如果沒有足夠的流動性來傳導，這個訊號就可能丟失。最終，在交易者看來，它可能被當作噪音而非真正的前瞻性訊號而忽略掉。

### 2026-05-29 20:34:07  #行業 #其他 #焦點

【國家網信辦等五部門聯合公佈《網際網路資訊內容多渠道分發服務管理規定》】《規定》明確，提供或者使用網際網路資訊內容多渠道分發服務，應當遵守法律、行政法規和國家有關規定，弘揚社會主義核心價值觀；鼓勵相關行業組織加強行業自律。《規定》要求，網際網路資訊內容多渠道分發服務機構應當依法辦理經營主體登記，並依法取得相應行政許可；平臺應當與網際網路資訊內容多渠道分發服務機構簽訂入駐協議，並及時將入駐情況報所在地省級網信部門備案；平臺應當以顯著方式在簽約網際網路使用者公眾賬號資訊頁面展示該賬號所屬網際網路資訊內容多渠道分發服務機構名稱。《規定》提出，網際網路資訊內容多渠道分發服務機構應當依法核實網際網路使用者公眾賬號生產運營者身份，依法依約履行資訊保安管理義務，協助並督促網際網路使用者公眾賬號生產運營者遵守資訊保安管理要求。《規定》鼓勵生產傳播正能量資訊內容，規定違法和不良資訊管理義務，明確禁止實施混淆視聽、誤導公眾等行為，擾亂網際網路資訊服務秩序或者謀取不正當利益；加強未成年人網路保護，明確不得為未滿十六週歲的未成年人提供網路直播發布相關服務；加強網路直播營銷活動管理，要求建立規範的直播商品選品機制、糾錯機制等。此外，《規定》還明確了相關違法行為的法律責任等。（網信中國）

### 2026-05-29 20:33:55  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Goods Trade Balance #Importance 2

US Goods Deficit Narrows in April — The US goods deficit narrowed to $82.4 billion in April 2026 from $85.3 billion in March, according to preliminary data. Exports surged 4% to a record $219.7 billion, driven by strong sales of capital goods (up 7.5%), industrial supplies (up 2.1%), and consumer goods (up 7.8%), though exports of vehicles and foods fell by 2.8% and 0.3%, respectively. Meanwhile, imports rose at a slower pace of 1.9% to $302.1 billion. Increases in capital goods (up 5.6%) and foods (up 0.3%) were partially offset by declines in industrial supplies (down 1.9%), vehicles (down 1.5%), and consumer goods (down 1.0%). For the January–April period, the goods deficit shrank sharply to $330 billion from $549 billion in the same period of 2025.

### 2026-05-29 20:10:52  #Trading Economics #Economy #Brazil #GDP Growth Rate #Importance 2

Brazil GDP Grows More than Expected — The Brazilian GDP expanded by 1.1% in the first quarter of 2026, gaining traction following the upwardly revised 0.3% increase in the previous quarter and slightly ahead of the market consensus of a 1% rise. It was the sharpest growth rate in one year, remaining supported by the elevated level of government spending that has boosted household consumption in recent quarters and drove the Brazilian central bank to maintain interest rates at an elevated level. Household consumption jumped by 1% in the period, and public expenditure inched higher by 0.4%. Meanwhile, gross fixed capital formation surged by 3.5%. On the other hand, net foreign demand had a negative contribution to GDP growth, with exports rising by 1.7% while imports surged by 4.4%.

### 2026-05-29 20:07:07  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Germany’s Inflation Eases to 2.6% in May — Germany’s consumer price inflation slowed to 2.6% year-over-year in May 2026, down from an over two-year high of 2.9% in April and slightly below market expectations, according to preliminary data. Despite the decline, inflation remains above the ECB’s 2% target. Goods inflation fell to 2.2% from 2.9%, driven by easing energy (6.6% vs. 10.1%) and food (0.4% vs. 1.2%) price pressures. However, services inflation accelerated to 3.1%, rebounding from a three-and-a-half-year low of 2.8% in April. Core inflation, excluding food and energy, rose to 2.5%, up from a five-year low. Meanwhile, the EU-harmonized annual inflation rate also eased to 2.7% from 2.9%.

Attendees include Chinese Communist Party General Secretary and President Su Lin, East Timor President Orta and U.S. Defense Secretary Pete Hegseth. Delegates are focusing on regional peace and security, with the Middle East’s impact on Asian energy and security highlighted as a key agenda item. 【國產儲存技術加速追趕 超級週期助力產業鏈升級】記者發現，在儲存器出口金額激增的背景下，國產儲存企業正經歷一場從“規模擴張”到“技術深耕”的深刻轉變。這一輪AI驅動的超級週期，能否成為中國儲存行業從“追趕”邁向“並跑”的關鍵轉折點？在儲存器供給緊張的背景下，不少企業也在積極擴產，記者從一家儲存企業瞭解到，他們原本的3000平方米的工廠已經擴充套件到了8000平方米，預計還會再增加四條產線，月產能將提升50%到150%之間，此外，他們也加大研發力度，針對不同行業的特性，推出定製化儲存器產品。 事實上，在儲存器產品出口高速增長的背後，國記憶體儲企業的技術創新和產業鏈升級也正在悄然提速。業內人士表示，這一輪AI驅動的“超級週期”，給國產儲存行業提供寶貴的“利潤反哺研發”視窗期，不少企業正抓住機遇，從“跟隨者”向“並跑者”轉變。 (央視財經)

【廣西玉林城區部分割槽域自來水發黃、發黑、有異味，自來水公司致歉】玉林市自來水有限公司5月29日釋出通告稱，5月29日0時許，二環北路嘉和小區附近DN1000供水主幹管突發爆管，險情緊急，為防止管網大面積失壓、漏水範圍擴大，公司立即關閉相關主幹管閥門實施搶修作業，造成連片供水管網管內壓力、水流流速驟變，致使管道內壁沉積物、鏽蝕物被沖刷進入水體，導致玉林城區部分割槽域出現自來水發黃、發黑、有異味等情況。為此給廣大市民帶來不便，深表歉意。 玉林市自來水有限公司表示，事件發生後，公司第一時間啟動應急響應，全力開展管網搶修、沖洗排汙、水質監測等工作。目前，管網搶修工作已完成，水質處置工作全力推進中，相關區域水質正在逐步恢復正常。 玉林市自來水有限公司稱，本次水質異常系主供水管老化突發爆管引發，屬突發事件，並非水源水質問題。下一步，該公司將持續做好後續處置，強化全流程水質監控，全力保障城市供水安全。

Brazil Budget Deficit Tops Forecasts — Brazil posted a nominal budget deficit of BRL 60.1 billion in April 2026, up from BRL 55.5 billion in the same month a year ago and above the expected BRL 50.7 billion shortfall. The central government gap rose to BRL 50.1 billion from BRL 44 billion in April 2025, and the state-owned companies' shortfall deficit increased to BRL 2.4 billion from BRL 1.7 billion a year ago. Meanwhile, the regional governments' shortfall shrank to BRL 7.6 billion from BRL 9.9 billion a year ago.

### 2026-05-29 19:41:01  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Brent Set for 17% Monthly Decline — Brent crude oil futures fell about 2% to $91.2 per barrel on Friday, the lowest in roughly six weeks, putting them on track for a 17% decline in May, the most since 2020. The move followed reports that the US and Iran have reached a preliminary agreement to extend a ceasefire and ease restrictions on shipping through the Strait of Hormuz, although President Donald Trump has not yet approved the deal and Iranian state media said it has not been finalized. The potential agreement has raised expectations of an eventual end to the US-Israeli war in Iran and a reopening of the Strait of Hormuz, a critical route for about one-fifth of global oil and LNG flows. Analysts warn that any recovery in flows would likely be slow, as mines would need clearing, damaged infrastructure repaired and shut-in production restarted, with tanker delays also limiting supply restoration.

【理想再調基座模型組織，新增 3 個具身智慧部門】5 月 29 日，理想汽車基座模型部門完成新一輪組織調整：新增 3 個具身智慧相關二級部門，分別為具身工程、具身互動、具身行為；同時自動駕駛變成獨立二級部門。與上述部門平行的，還有資料引擎、模擬與測評等部門。調整後，自動駕駛、具身工程、具身行為 3 個部門直接由基座模型負責人詹錕管理，向理想汽車 CTO 謝炎彙報。這是理想今年第二次調整與智慧駕駛、具身智慧相關的組織。今年 2 月 14 日，理想重組了智慧化部門，形成人形機器人、軟體本體、基座模型三個團隊。基座模型團隊主要統籌自研的智駕晶片馬赫 M100、與智駕 VLA 司機大模型。人形機器人團則隊聚焦機器人，該部門最早由理想前自動駕駛研發高階副總裁郎鹹朋負責。郎鹹朋今年 2 月離職後，最新部門負責人為湛逸飛，向公司總裁、智慧汽車戰略負責人馬東輝彙報。（晚點）

### 2026-05-29 19:19:28  #其他

【國家醫保局公佈新一批價格招採“嚴重或特別嚴重失信”企業】受國家醫療保障局委託，國家醫療保障局醫藥價格和招標採購指導中心彙總了截至2026年4月1日各省份評級為“特別嚴重失信”和“嚴重失信”的200餘家醫藥企業情況。價格招採信用評級主要依據行賄金額、不正當價格行為、擾亂集中採購秩序等嚴重程度進行確定。比如，累計行賄金額1萬元以上為“失信”，累計行賄金額50萬元以上為“嚴重失信”，累計行賄金額100萬元以上為“特別嚴重失信”。根據醫藥企業信用評級，可採取提醒告誡、提示風險、披露失信資訊，直至限制或中止該企業涉案或全部藥品醫用耗材掛網、投標或配送資格等處置措施。比如，對“嚴重（失信）”評級企業，採取取消涉案產品掛網和配送資格等處置措施；對“特別嚴重（失信）”評級企業，採取取消其所有產品在本省（涉案產品全國範圍內）的掛網和配送資格等處置措施。（央視新聞）

【陳文清在俄出席第十四屆安全事務高階代表國際會議】中共中央政治局委員、中央政法委書記陳文清28日在俄羅斯莫斯科出席第十四屆安全事務高階代表國際會議並作主旨發言。145個國家和國際組織的代表參加會議。 陳文清說，當前世界進入新的動盪變革期，霸權主義與冷戰思維甚囂塵上，經濟全球化遭遇逆流，新興技術帶來的安全風險上升。習近平主席提出的全球安全倡議，倡導以團結精神適應正在深刻調整的國際格局，以共贏思維應對各種傳統安全和非傳統安全風險挑戰，在推動解決全球安全難題方面發揮日益重要的作用。中國願與各國共同深入踐行全球安全倡議，共建人類命運共同體，攜手邁向持久和平和普遍安全。 陳文清表示，我們要共同維護世界和平，堅決反對任何形式的霸權主義和強權政治。共同遵守國際法治，全面、充分、完整遵守聯合國憲章宗旨，維護國際法治的公正性和穩定性。共同踐行多邊主義，尊重各國平等參與國際和地區安全事務的權利。今年，中國將舉辦亞太經合組織領導人非正式會議等重大活動，期待同各方加強協調，實現合作共贏。 陳文清還出席了金磚國家代表團團長午餐會，並與包括俄羅斯聯邦安全會議秘書紹伊古在內的多國代表團團長舉行雙邊會談會見。(新華社)

Macau Jobless Rate Edges Up to 1.8% — The unemployment rate in Macau edged up to 1.8% in the three months ending in April 2026, compared to 1.6% in the previous period. The number of unemployed persons increased by 300 from the previous month to 6,500, while total employment declined by 2,400 to 374,600 people. Among local residents, employment gains were mainly recorded in hotels and related activities, as well as transport and storage, while job losses were seen in wholesale and retail trade, real estate and business activities, and gaming. The general labor force participation rate fell to 66.4% from 66.7% in the preceding period. By gender, participation rates among local residents declined for both men (64.1% vs. 64.4%) and women (59.0% vs. 59.3%).

### 2026-05-29 18:40:49  #Trading Economics #Economy #Portugal #GDP Growth Rate #Importance 1

Portugal Economy Stalls Amid Weather Shocks and Middle East Fallout — Portugal’s economy stalled in the first quarter of 2026, marking a sharp slowdown from the 0.9% growth recorded in the previous period, particularly driven by extreme weather, including Storm Kristin and heavy rainfall, which damaged infrastructure, agriculture, and buildings, particularly in the Central region, as well as the adverse effects of the Middle East conflict. The slowdown also reflected the fading impact of government measures that had boosted household income in the second half of 2025. Household consumption growth plummeted to 0.1% in Q1 2026 from 0.9%, while government spending rose by 0.3%, down from 0.4%. Net external demand weighed on GDP, as exports grew by 2.1% but imports surged at a faster pace of 5.4%. On a positive note, fixed investment increased by 1.9%, following a 1.2% gain in the previous quarter. The Bank of Portugal warned that rising inflation and tighter financing conditions are likely to constrain consumption and investment throughout 2026.

Macedonia Unemployment Rate Continues to Fall — North Macedonia’s unemployment rate fell to 11.3% in the first quarter of 2026, its lowest level since at least 1993, compared with 11.4% in the previous period. The number of unemployed declined by 1% to 90,499, of whom 62.4% were men and 37.6% women. Meanwhile, the number of employed persons edged down by 0.3% to 707,385, with men accounting for 57.9% and women for 42.1%. The employment rate stood at 46.5%. Informal employment rose to 94,197, with the largest share concentrated in agriculture, forestry and fisheries (43.6%), followed by construction (17.5%).

### 2026-05-29 18:38:23  #Trading Economics #Economy #Latvia #Retail Sales YoY #Importance 1

Latvia Retail Sales Growth at 4-Month Low — Retail sales in Latvia increased 2.6% year-on-year in April 2026, easing from an upwardly revised 4.2% in the previous month, marking the weakest growth since December 2025. Sales slowed for non-food products, except for retail sale of automotive fuel (2.9% vs 5.4% in March), as well as for communication equipment in specialized stores (5.3% vs 11.9%), hardware, instruments, construction materials and sanitary equipment (5.2% vs 11.1%), cultural and recreation goods (5.9% vs 12.4%), and automotive fuel (3% vs 6.6%). Sales also declined in non-specialised stores (-1.6% vs 1.8%) and clothing, footwear, and leather goods (-8.8% vs 3.7%). In contrast, retail activity rose in electrical household appliances (3% vs 1.9%), pharmaceutical and medical goods (9.2% vs 2.7%), cosmetic and toilet articles (2.9% vs 2.2%), and sales via mail order houses or via the Internet (4.5% vs 0.7%). On a monthly basis, retail sales fell 0.4% in April, deepening from a 0.1% drop in March.

### 2026-05-29 18:27:04  #Trading Economics #Economy #Ireland #Harmonised Inflation Rate YoY #Importance 1

Ireland Harmonised Inflation Edged Down to 3.5% — The EU-standard Harmonised Index of Consumer Prices (HICP) in Ireland rose by 3.5% year-on-year in May 2026, easing slightly from a 3.6% rise in March, according to preliminary estimates. Energy prices are estimated to have eased to 11.9% from 15.5% in the previous month. Food inflation also softened, slowing to 1.4% from 2.0%. In contrast, services inflation accelerated to 3.7% from 3.3%. Excluding energy and unprocessed food inflation is is estimated to have edged up to 2.7% from 2.6% in April. On a monthly basis, harmonised consumer prices fell 0.1% in May, reversing the 0.4% increase in the previous month.

### 2026-05-29 18:23:30  #Trading Economics #Economy #Italy #GDP Growth Rate #Importance 1

Italy GDP Growth Revised Higher — The Italian GDP expanded by 0.3% in the first quarter of 2026, maintaining the pace of growth from the earlier period and revised above the 0.2% increase penned in the preliminary estimate, further beating expectations of a 0.1% growth rate. The GDP was carried by a 0.4% expansion in domestic expenditure, with household consumption rising 0.5% while public expenditure stalled. In the meantime, gross fixed investment expanded by 0.7%, as a 2.7% drop in residential development was offset by a 1.3% increase in non-residential investments. Meanwhile, net foreign demand contributed to a 0.9 percentage point increase in the GDP growth figure, as exports grew by 2.2% in the period while imports fell by 0.7%. This offset a 1.1 percentage point drag on the GDP growth figure from negative changes to inventory. From the previous year, the first quarter GDP expanded by 0.8%.

### 2026-05-29 18:18:43  #Trading Economics #Economy #Portugal #Unemployment Rate #Importance 1

Portugal Unemployment Rate Edges Down in April — Portugal’s unemployment rate edged down to 5.7% in April 2026 from 5.8% in each of the previous two months, and compared with 6.2% a year earlier. The number of unemployed stood at 322,000, a decrease of 3,300 from the previous month. A persistent gender gap remained, with the unemployment rate for women at 6.1%, compared with 5.3% for men. Youth unemployment fell to 17.9%, its lowest level since October 2022. Meanwhile, the employment rate edged up to 66.2%, the highest since February 1998, from 66.0% in the previous month, with the number of employed rising by 23,000 to 5.344 million. The inactivity rate declined to 29.8% from 30.0%, while the labour force increased by 19,900 to 5.666 million. The labour underutilisation rate stood at 9.7%, down from 9.8% in the previous month.

Latvia Q1 GDP Growth Eases to 2.5% — The economy of Latvia grew by 2.5% year-on-year in the first quarter of 2026, easing from a 2.9% expansion in the previous quarter. The slowdown was mainly driven by a weaker performances in manufacturing (1.1% vs 6.8%), construction (2.8% vs 10%), human health and social work activities (2.1% vs 4.4%), and arts, entertainment, and recreation (2.7% vs 3.3%). Additionally, accommodation and food services (-1.8% vs 0.4%), information and communication (-2.4% vs 7.4%), and public administration and defence (-0.9% vs 0.9%) recorded declines, while activity declined further in transportation (-8.3% vs -5.7%). In contrast, performances strengthened in trade (5.5% vs 2.6%), real estate (3.7% vs 0.5%), and other industries (21.6% vs 2.3%). On a seasonally adjusted quarterly basis, GDP expanded 0.6% in Q1, following an upwardly revised 0.7% growth in the preceding period.

### 2026-05-29 18:15:16  #Trading Economics #Economy #Luxembourg #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Luxembourg Producer Prices Fall at Softer Pace — Producer prices in Luxembourg declined 5.4% year-on-year in April 2026, easing from a revised 7.8% drop in the previous month and marking the ninth consecutive month of contraction. The slower pace of decline reflected easing deflation across key categories, including consumer goods (-13.5% vs -15.9% in March), energy goods (-8.3% vs -19.1%), and intermediate goods (-3.8% vs -4.1%). Conversely, inflation for capital goods increased (1.7% vs 1.3%). On a monthly basis, producer prices rose 1.2% in April, following a 0.8% increase in the prior month.

Portugal Inflation Rate Steady at 3.3% — The annual inflation rate in Portugal was unchanged at 3.3% in May 2026, remaining at the highest since September 2023, preliminary estimates showed. A faster increase in energy products (13.2% vs 11.7% in April) was offset by a slowdown in cost of unprocessed food (5.7% vs 7.5%). The core inflation index, which excludes energy and unprocessed food products components was 2.2%, the same value as in the previous month. On a monthly basis, consumer prices increased 0.3%, after a 1.4% rise in April. The EU Harmonised Index of Consumer Prices annual rate of change was 3.1% (3.3% in the previous month).

【河北立法保障雄安新區軌道快線運營安全】記者從河北省人大常委會法工委獲悉，《河北雄安新區軌道快線運營安全條例》將於今年7月1日起施行，該條例是京津冀首部跨區域軌道交通運營安全管理的地方性法規。 京雄快線專案起自雄安新區啟動區城市航站樓，與北京大興機場線貫通運營，接入北京地鐵網，是雄安新區“四縱兩橫”區域軌道交通網路中的關鍵“一縱”。線路全長約86公里，設站8座，是設計時速200公里、採用公交化運營模式的軌道快線。日前，京雄快線已進入聯調聯試階段。 “跨區域、功能複合、高速大運量”是京雄快線專案的一大特點。為此，條例立足軌道快線跨省運營屬性，建立京冀常態化溝通協調機制，推動運營監管有效覆蓋、治理責任貫通；統一兩地應急預案、聯合演練、應急處置、資訊釋出要求；細化大客流疏導、突發事件先期處置、應急設施配備、緊急疏散引導等，提升跨區域管理和應急處置一體化水平。（新華社）

### 2026-05-29 18:01:19  #其他 #焦點

【中央網信辦等四部門印發《2026年提升全民數字素養與技能工作要點》】近日，中央網信辦、教育部、工業和資訊化部、人力資源社會保障部聯合印發《2026年提升全民數字素養與技能工作要點》（以下簡稱《工作要點》）。《工作要點》部署了6個方面15項重點任務。一是完善數字素養培育體系，包括強化數字資源供給開放、拓寬數字素養提升渠道。二是深化數字應用場景建設，包括提升數字生活品質、提升數字工作能力、激發數字創新活力。三是提升全民人工智慧素養，包括強化人工智慧賦能教育、加快人工智慧人才培育、深化人工智慧普及應用。四是促進數字普惠包容發展，包括深化資訊無障礙環境建設、打造數字助老惠民公益專案。五是營造安全有序網路空間，包括引導全民文明依法上網用網、築牢全民網路安全防護意識、促進人工智慧安全規範發展。六是健全協同聯動工作機制，包括完善多方協作機制、深化國際交流合作。（網信辦）

Regional progress: Northeast >90%, Huanghuaihai ~90%, Southwest >80%, other regions near completion. ### 2026-05-29 17:51:34  #Trading Economics #Economy #Sri Lanka #Producer Prices Change #Importance 0

Sri Lanka Producer Inflation Accelerates in April — Producer prices in Sri Lanka rose by 1.7% year-on-year in April 2026, picking up from a 0.5% rise in the previous month. The acceleration was mainly driven by a sharp rebound in electricity, gas, steam and air conditioning supply (17.2% vs -4.6% in March).Prices for water collection, treatment, and supply also rose at a faster pace (5.3% vs 0.6%). Additionally, inflation increased further for manufacturing (4.3% vs 1.3%), supported by higher costs of chemical, non-metallic mineral, other transport equipment, and food products. In contrast, agricultural producer prices declined at a much steeper pace (-15.2% vs 1.6%), largely due to weaker prices for perennial and non-perennial crops. On a monthly basis, producer prices advanced by 1.1% in April.

### 2026-05-29 17:48:47  #Trading Economics #Economy #Macau #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Macau Trade Gap Increases in April — Macau's trade deficit widened to MOP 11.761 billion in April 2026, up by MOP 2.64 billion from the same month in the previous year. Exports increased 20.9% year-on-year to MOP 1.359 billion, with re-exports which account for about 90% of total exports, also rising 20.9%. Sales increased the most for machines, apparatus and parts (25.3%), diamond & diamond jewellery (22.4%) and watches (21.7%). Meanwhile, domestic exports went up by 21.3%, led by copper & articles thereof (56.9%). Most exports went to Hong Kong and China. Imports increased 28% to MOP 13.12 billion, with purchases of gold jewellery jumping 39.4% while those of food & beverages declined 11.7%. Most purchases came from China and the EU. Considering the first four months of 2026, merchandise exports and imports rose by 20% and 25.7% respectively year-on-year. The trade deficit increased by MOP 9.36 billion to MOP 44.77 billion.

### 2026-05-29 17:48:05  #Trading Economics #Economy #Greece #Unemployment Rate #Importance 1

Greece Jobless Rate Eases in April — The seasonally adjusted unemployment rate in Greece eased to 9.5% in April 2026 from an upwardly revised 10.4% in the previous month, marking the lowest level since January. The number of unemployed individuals fell by 49.5 thousand from a month earlier to 452.3 thousand but increased by 26.6 thousand year-on-year. Unemployment remained significantly higher among women than men, with the rate at 13.7% for women and 5.9% for men. Meanwhile, the number of people outside the labour force, those under 75 who are neither employed nor actively seeking work rose by 52 thousand month-on-month to 2,950 thousand but declined by 57.1 thousand year-on-year. In addition, employment fell by 9.9 thousand from the prior month to 4,523 thousand.

### 2026-05-29 17:43:56  #Trading Economics #Economy #Sri Lanka #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Sri Lanka Inflation Rate Rise the Most Since 2024 — The inflation rate in Colombo, Sri Lanka’s capital, edged up 5.5% in May 2026 from 5.4% in the previous month. This marked the highest reading since February 2024, as non-food prices rose by 7.8% from 6.8% in April, lifted by cost increases in transport, housing and utilities, restaurants and hotels, education, miscellaneous goods, health, and household equipment. Meanwhile, food inflation moderated sharply to 0.9% from 2.8%. On a monthly basis, consumer prices grew by 0.9% in May, easing from a 3% growth in the preceding period.

### 2026-05-29 17:42:45  #Trading Economics #Economy #Belgium #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Belgium Producer Inflation at 2023-Highs — Producer prices in Belgium surged by 8.7% year-on-year in April 2026, accelerating sharply from a 3.5% increase in March and marking the strongest rise since March 2023. The acceleration was driven by both domestic prices, which climbed 8.2% after rising 3.7% in March, and non-domestic prices, which jumped 9.7% following a 3.3% increase. Price pressures were broad-based across industries, led by manufacturing, where producer prices surged 9.3%, up significantly from 4.7% in the previous month. Inflation rebounded for mining and quarrying (0.6% vs -0.1%) and electricity, gas, steam and air conditioning supply (2.5% vs -9.1%). Meanwhile, inflation stood for water supply, sewerage, waste management, and remediation activities at 4.2%. On a monthly basis, producer prices rose 2.9% in April, mainly driven by a 7.4% increase in energy prices. Excluding energy, producer prices were up 1.7% from the previous month.

### 2026-05-29 17:39:51  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

German Bund Yields Near April Lows as Inflation Concerns Ease — German 10-year Bund yields dipped to 2.95% at the end of May, approaching their lowest level since April 8 and heading for an eight-basis-point monthly decline. Cautious optimism over a potential US-Iran agreement helped ease inflation concerns, as negotiations to end the three-month war continued. Reports suggested Washington and Tehran had agreed on a 60-day ceasefire extension to allow formal talks, though President Trump has yet to approve it. Investors also analyzed fresh inflation data from Europe’s largest economies for clues on the ECB’s policy direction. German regional CPI data indicated national inflation eased in May despite higher energy prices linked to the Iran war. Meanwhile, inflation rates in France, Spain, and Italy accelerated to multi-year highs, all exceeding the ECB’s 2% target. ECB meeting minutes revealed some policymakers would have supported an April rate hike if proposed, reinforcing expectations of a 25-basis-point increase at the June 11 meeting.

### 2026-05-29 17:24:05  #Trading Economics #Economy #Greece #Retail Sales YoY #Importance 1

Greece Retail Sales Growth at 4-Month Low — Retail sales in Greece increased 3.0% year-on-year in March 2026, slowing from a downwardly revised 4.4% rise in the previous month and marking the weakest growth since November 2025. Sales growth eased for food (2.9% vs 7.4% in February) and the non-food sector excluding automotive fuel (5.1% vs 8.9%), while sales of automotive fuel fell at a softer pace (-0.3% vs -3.1%). Among specialized categories, retail activity also moderated in supermarkets (2.7% vs 5.7%), food, beverages, and tobacco (0.6% vs 11.4%), pharmaceutical products and cosmetics (3.9% vs 10.2%), furniture, electrical equipment and household goods (12.5% vs 17.3%), and books, stationery and other goods (6.9% vs 7.1%). At the same time, sales declined in department stores (-0.3% vs 2.7%), and fell further for clothing and footwear (-3% vs -0.5%). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, retail activity fell 0.5% in April.

【三星電子、SK海力士屢創新高觸發意外拋售 今年合計淨流出資金達586億美元】全球投資者今年瘋狂湧入三星電子和SK海力士股票交易，推動這兩隻記憶體股分別上漲了147%和245%，但飆升的股價給一些基金帶來了意想不到的麻煩。由於這兩家公司股價屢創新高，已經觸及了一些基金單隻股票持倉比例上限10%的限制，迫使這些基金拋售這兩隻股票。蘇黎世的GAM投資管理公司和新加坡的Jupiter資產管理公司據悉正是觸發此類拋售的公司之一，其已經無奈調整了投資組合。這一被迫拋售無疑凸顯了當前記憶體市場的擁擠程度，也可能進一步加大波動性。截至週四，全球投資者淨拋售了價值636億美元的韓國股票，這是自1999年有資料以來最大的單月拋售量。其中，三星和SK海力士今年合計淨流出資金達586億美元。

### 2026-05-29 17:16:14  #Trading Economics #Economy #Iceland #GDP Annual Growth Rate #Importance 1

Iceland Economy Returns to Growth in Q1 — Iceland’s economy expanded 2.7% year-on-year in the first quarter of 2026, rebounding from a 0.6% contraction in the previous quarter. Net trade contributed positively to GDP, with exports rising 1% after a 5% decline, as services growth accelerated (6.9% vs 4.3%) and goods exports declined at a slower pace (-3.9% vs -13.3%). Meanwhile, imports fell further by 8% following a 4.2% decline. At the same time, gross capital formation contracted less sharply (-12% vs -12.5%), while changes in inventories rose 1.1% after stagnating in Q4 2025. In contrast, government spending growth eased to 0.8% from 1.1%, while consumer spending slowed to 2.2% from 5.5%. On a seasonally adjusted quarterly basis, GDP jumped 3.7%, recovering from a 0.8% contraction in the preceding period.

【濟南市委原常委孫斌嚴重違紀違法被開除黨籍和公職】山東省紀委監委29日通報，經山東省委批准，山東省紀委監委對濟南市委原常委，濟南高新技術產業開發區黨工委原書記、管委會原主任孫斌嚴重違紀違法問題進行了立案審查調查。 經查，孫斌喪失理想信念，背棄初心使命，對黨不忠誠不老實，對抗組織審查；無視中央八項規定精神，違規收受消費卡，接受可能影響公正執行公務的宴請；違背組織原則，隱瞞不報個人有關事項，在幹部選拔任用工作中違規為他人謀取利益；違反廉潔紀律，向管理和服務物件放貸獲取大額回報；違規干預和插手市場經濟活動；將黨和人民賦予的公權力異化為謀取私利的工具，大肆收錢斂財，利用職務便利為他人在工程專案承攬、企業經營等方面謀利，並非法收受鉅額財物。 孫斌嚴重違反黨的政治紀律、組織紀律、廉潔紀律和工作紀律，構成嚴重職務違法並涉嫌受賄犯罪，且在黨的十八大後不收斂、不收手，性質嚴重，影響惡劣，應予嚴肅處理。依據《中國共產黨紀律處分條例》《中華人民共和國監察法》《中華人民共和國公職人員政務處分法》等有關規定，經山東省紀委常委會會議研究並報山東省委批准，決定給予孫斌開除黨籍處分；由山東省監委給予其開除公職處分；終止其濟南市第十二次黨代會代表資格；收繳其違紀違法所得；將其涉嫌犯罪問題移送檢察機關依法審查起訴，所涉財物一併移送。（新華社）

### 2026-05-29 17:12:37  #Trading Economics #Economy #Italy #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Italy Inflation Rate Rises to Over 2-Year High — The Italian headline inflation rate rose to 3.2% in May of 2026 from 2.7% in the previous month, aligning with the market consensus to mark the highest inflation rate since September of 2023. The increase was consistent with that from other large Eurozone economies as tight oil an gas supply since the outbreak of war in the Middle East lifted price growth across energy importers. Prices accelerated for both non-regulated energy (12.6% vs 9.6% in April) and regulated energy prices (5.8% vs 5.3%). Likewise, inflation was higher for transportation services (1.8% vs 0.6%) and recreational and cultural services (3% vs 2.6%). Core inflation, which excludes food and energy prices, rose to 1.8% from 1.6% in the earlier period. From the previous month, the Italian CPI rose by 0.4%, slowing from the 1.1% increase in April.

### 2026-05-29 17:11:51  #Trading Economics #Economy #Croatia #Retail Sales YoY #Importance 1

Croatia Retail Sales Growth Eases in April — Retail sales in Croatia rose by 2.0% year-on-year in April 2026, easing from a 3.3% increase in the previous month. This marked the softest growth since November last year, as sales increased at a slower pace for food, beverages, and tobacco (2.6% vs 3.2% in March) and for non-food products excluding automotive fuels and lubricants (2.8% vs 8.0%). At the same time, sales of automotive fuels and lubricants declined sharply (-15.1% vs -5.0%), while retail sales via the internet or mail order turned negative (-3.6% vs 3.7%). Meanwhile, retail trade excluding automotive fuels and lubricants in specialised stores rose 3.9%, following a 4.6% increase in March. On a monthly basis, retail trade fell 0.2%, after a 0.9% rise in March. For the January–April period, retail sales went up by 2.8% compared with the same period a year earlier.

### 2026-05-29 17:11:38  #Trading Economics #Economy #Croatia #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Croatia Industrial Output Declines in April — Croatia’s industrial production dropped by 2.1% year-on-year in April 2026, reversing a 0.8% increase in the previous month. This was the sharpest fall since November 2024 due to lower output in manufacturing (-1.5% vs 1.8% in March), mining and quarrying (-0.7% vs 3.3%), and electricity, gas, steam and air-conditioning supply (-11.3% vs -6.2%). Within the manufacturing sector, notable declines were recorded in other transport equipment (-28.3% vs 27.2%), textiles (-16.0% vs 3.0%), wearing apparel (-14.3% vs 3.7%), leather and related products (-16.2% vs 6.4%), and other non-metallic mineral products (-14.9% vs 7.4%). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, industrial production decreased by 0.6%, following a revised 1.1% decline in March.

【Shenzhou-22 enters flyaround phase; three-orbit autonomous fast-return planned】At 14:44 on May 29 Shenzhou-22 successfully detached from the Chinese space station complex and began a flyaround, Beijing mission control said. The Shenzhou-21 crew will return to the Dongfeng landing site aboard Shenzhou-22. Controllers described the departure as smooth; during withdrawal the vehicle will make brief holds at two points 19 m and 200 m from the station. Shenzhou-22 is executing a three-orbit autonomous fast‑return profile, as used for Shenzhou-21, with a total return time of roughly 5.5 hours. Altitude will be reduced progressively during the flyaround; after three orbits, two attitude adjustments and separation maneuvers the spacecraft will reenter and proceed to final descent.

### 2026-05-29 16:59:54  #其他

【5月人民幣對美元即期匯率累計升值0.95%】德邦證券在研報中指出，表面上看，人民幣升值與美元階段性回落有關，但如果人民幣只是在美元走弱背景下被動修復，那麼其對一籃子貨幣的表現未必會同步改善。該機構認為，本輪人民幣升值並不只是外部美元因素變化後的匯率反彈，也反映了外部風險緩和、出口韌性延續、順差支撐仍強以及結匯意願改善之後，人民幣自身定價的邊際修復。展望後續，該機構指出，短期看，人民幣匯率修復、外部風險緩和和低估值狀態疊加，有助於改善中國資產的定價環境，港股中資資產、外資定價權重較高的龍頭資產以及此前受風險偏好壓制較多的板塊，或更容易先出現修復。但中長期來看，匯率本身並不能決定資產重估的高度和持續性。真正決定中國資產能否從階段性估值修復走向系統性重估的，仍然是盈利改善能否持續、企業經營正迴圈能否延續，以及政策預期能否保持穩定。（澎湃新聞）

【中國歐盟商會與歐洲議會對華關係代表團一行舉行會見】5月28日，中國歐盟商會主席彥辭與顧問委員會代表，在北京會見了歐洲議會對華關係代表團團長恩金·埃羅格魯一行。此次會見由歐盟駐華大使庹堯誨主辦，埃羅格魯主持。會上，彥辭分享了中國與歐盟、以及中國與其他地區間的一些貿易資料。隨後，他概述了商會同日釋出的《商業信心調查2026》的主要結論。他指出，在華歐洲企業的商業信心或已達到拐點：調查資料顯示認為過去一年在華營商更加艱難的受訪企業比例有所下降。但他同時強調，中國經濟增長放緩、不可持續的競爭以及普遍存在的市場準入和監管壁壘等結構性挑戰依然存在。彥辭總結道，中國有望透過優先垂手可得的成果，實現商業信心回暖。彥辭發言後，會議進入問答環節，商會其他代表也分別從各自行業視角分享了在華營商的相關見解。

UK Gilt Yields Near April Lows as Inflation Fears Ease — UK 10-year gilt yields hovered just above 4.8% at the end of May, near their lowest since April 17 and on track for a 19-basis-point monthly decline, as cautious optimism over a potential US-Iran agreement helped ease inflation concerns. Negotiations to end the three-month war continued, with reports that Washington and Tehran had agreed on a 60-day ceasefire extension to allow formal talks, though President Trump has yet to approve the memorandum of understanding. The bond market also drew support from UK Prime Minister Keir Starmer’s Labour Party suffering local election losses that were not as severe as initially feared. Domestic data further bolstered sentiment, with a cooling labor market, softer-than-expected inflation, and signs of slowing economic activity leading traders to scale back expectations for a Bank of England rate hike.

### 2026-05-29 16:53:57  #公司

【長春高新：甲磺酸亮丙瑞林注射液（6M）上市申請獲得受理】長春高新公告，近日，控股子公司金賽藥業收到國家藥品監督管理局核准簽發的《受理通知書》，甲磺酸亮丙瑞林注射液（6M）境外生產藥品註冊上市許可申請獲得受理。該藥品為一種促性腺激素釋放激素激動劑（GnRHa）長效製劑，適應症為乳腺癌。此次申報上市是基於一項在中國絕經前乳腺癌患者中開展的關鍵臨床研究（GenSci093-301），主要終點為給藥後第4周至第48週期間血清雌二醇（E2）維持在絕經後水平的患者比例。研究結果表明，甲磺酸亮丙瑞林注射液（6M）對比抑那通®11.25mg（3M）在抑制E2至絕經水平方面效果相當。在實現相同治療療效的前提下，6M製劑將給藥間隔從12周延長至24周，顯著減少患者注射頻率，提高治療依從性，展現出明確的臨床優勢。

### 2026-05-29 16:46:35  #Trading Economics #Economy #Slovenia #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Slovenia Inflation Rate at 2024-Highs — Slovenia's annual inflation rate accelerate to 3.6% in May 2026, marking the highest level since March 2024, up from 3.1% in April. The increase was driven primarily by stronger price pressures in housing and utilities, where inflation rose to 10.0% from 9.7%, and transport, which accelerated to 5.5% from 4.6%. Additional upward pressure came from receation and culture (2.1% vs 1.1%), alcoholic beverages and tobacco (5.2% vs 4.3%), furnishings (1.9% vs 1.5%) and communications (2.5% vs 2.2%). Meanwhile, prices for clothing and footwear nearly stabilized, falling just 0.1% after a 2.9% decline in April. In contrast, eased slightly for food and non-alcoholic beverages (0.9% vs. 1.0%), health services (4.8% vs. 5.3%) and restaurants and hotels (2.2% vs 2.6%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 0.5% in May, slowing sharply from a 1.9% rise, largely due to lower prices for liquid fuels (-5.5%) and diesel (-4.5%). Harmonized inflation rose to 3.8% in May, up from 3.4% in April.

Cotton Futures Hover Around 1-Month Lows — Cotton futures traded around 77 cents per pound, near the lowest since mid-April, pressured by falling oil prices along with signs of ample supply and a stronger production outlook. Latest data showed ICE certified stocks increased by 104 bales to 225,259, the highest level since July 2017. Meanwhile, weather conditions across the US cotton belt have improved, especially in West Texas, easing drought concerns and improving crop prospects. The USDA reported US cotton planting at 53% complete as of May 24, ahead of last year’s 50% pace and slightly above the five-year average, reinforcing expectations for stable to higher supplies. Separately, US Agriculture Secretary announced the “Great American Cotton Plan” aimed at strengthening the US cotton sector and support producers. Elsewhere, consulting firm StoneX kept its Brazil's 2025/26 cotton production estimate unchanged at 3.858 million tons, while raising its export forecast by 6.5% to a record of 3.3 million tons.

### 2026-05-29 16:38:54  #Trading Economics #Economy #Philippines #Business Confidence #Importance 1

Philippines Business Morale Falls Further in April — The Business Confidence Index in the Philippines fell further to -35.8 in April 2026 from -24.3 in the previous month, marking the second consecutive month of decline. The pessimistic sentiment was driven by the US-Iran conflict, raising concerns that higher inflation could increase firms’ operating costs and erode households’ purchasing power. During the month, sentiment continued to deteriorate regarding financial conditions (-35.3 vs -24.9 in March), order books (-15.6 vs -2.7), and access to credit (-9.9 vs -8.1), while the volume of business activity fell sharply to -15.3 from a 2.2 increase in the prior month. Meanwhile, the business outlook declined at a softer pace (-7.5 vs -17.3), while confidence for the coming year improved (19.5 vs 11.7), supported by expectations of stronger demand, higher sales and income, better economic conditions, and a possible resolution of the Middle East conflict.

### 2026-05-29 16:37:32  #Trading Economics #Economy #Taiwan #GDP Annual Growth Rate #Importance 1

Taiwan Q1 GDP Growth Hits Multi-Decade High — Taiwan’s economy expanded by 14.55% year-on-year in Q1 2026, accelerating from an upwardly revised 12.95% in Q4 2025 and surpassing preliminary estimates of 13.69%. It marked the strongest economic growth since Q3 1978, supported by robust demand for AI technologies and related infrastructure, despite lingering risks from Middle East tensions that could threaten momentum. Domestic demand surged to 5.0% from 1.08% in Q4, primarily driven by stronger household consumption (4.74% vs 3.08%) and government spending (4.06% vs 0.77%). Gross fixed capital formation also accelerated (6.21% vs 4.28%). On the external front, exports of goods and services eased but remained strong (35.76% vs 40.02% in Q4), while imports of goods and services increased further (26.34% vs 25.88%). On a seasonally adjusted quarterly basis, Taiwan’s economy grew by 1.69% in Q1 2026, slowing sharply from an upwardly revised 6.51% expansion in the previous quarter.

### 2026-05-29 16:34:56  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Unemployment Rate #Importance 2

Germany’s Unemployment Dips in May — Germany’s seasonally adjusted unemployment rate fell to 6.3% in May 2026, down from a near six-year high of 6.4% in March and April, slightly beating market expectations. However, the Federal Employment Agency attributed the decline to a one-off effect. The number of unemployed persons decreased by 12,000 to 2.987 million, defying forecasts of a 10,000 rise. On a non-adjusted basis, unemployment dropped below 3 million to 2.95 million, largely due to a rebound following weak April data. Andrea Nahles, head of the agency, noted that "despite the decline, the spring upturn has failed to gain real momentum this year." Unemployment is expected to rise in the coming months as companies delay staffing adjustments in response to geopolitical shocks, including the Iran conflict.

### 2026-05-29 16:26:28  #Trading Economics #Economy #Bulgaria #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Bulgaria Producer Prices See Strongest Rise in a Year — Producer prices in Bulgaria rose by 15.2% year-on-year in April 2026, accelerating sharply from an 8.1% increase in March and marking the fastest pace since March 2025. The increase was driven primarily by mining and quarrying, which surged 64.3% (vs 40.4% in March), supported by a jump in mining of metal ores (115.2% vs 70.4%) and other mining and quarrying (5.3% vs 4.6%). Manufacturing inflation also picked up, rising 7.5% (vs 4.9%), with stronger increases in basic metals (19.8% vs 13.4%), and chemicals and chemical products (12.7% vs 4.9%. Energy-related pressures intensified notably, as electricity, gas, steam and air conditioning supply climbed 16.6% (vs 0.8%). By main industrial groups, intermediate goods rose 16.5% (vs 10.9%), while energy goods surged 28.4% (vs 6.3%). On a monthly basis, producer prices increased 1.9%, rebounding from a 0.1% decline in March.

### 2026-05-29 16:25:55  #Trading Economics #Economy #Bulgaria #Business Confidence #Importance 1

Bulgaria Business Mood Hits 11-Month High — Bulgaria’s industrial business climate index climbed to 18.4 in May 2026, picking up from a more than five-year low of 12.4 in the previous month. The reading was the highest since June 2025, as firms assessed the current business situation more favorably (23.2 vs 14.2 in April), despite present production activity turning negative (-2.2 vs 3.3). At the same time, expectations for production activity over the next three months improved (11.4 vs 9.4), while the employment outlook for the same period returned to positive territory (2.5 vs -0.1). Meanwhile, selling price expectations for the next three months eased (14.7 vs 18.9). Key limiting factors included economic uncertainty (48.4), labor shortages (32.7), weak domestic (23.7) and foreign (22.0) demand, as well as weaknesses in economic legislation (14.2).

Spain Current Account Surplus Widens in March — Spain's current account surplus widened to EUR 4.61 billion in March 2026, from EUR 3.01 billion in the same month last year, according to preliminary estimates. The balance of goods and services recorded a EUR 6.01 billion surplus, increasing 44.3% from EUR 4.17 billion a year earlier. 有記者提問，我們注意到，習近平主席在覆信參加“共航蔚藍：中美青年友誼行”的兩國學生時，宣佈“5年5萬”倡議提前兩年半時間實現預期目標。發言人能否介紹相關情況？

### 2026-05-29 16:19:01  #MKT News #Market Regions #Greater China

【MoF, SAMR issue guidance to advance high-quality development of local government procurement e-marketplaces】The Ministry of Finance and the State Administration for Market Regulation issued guidance to advance high-quality development of local government procurement e-marketplaces. The guidance defines e-marketplaces’ role: primarily to host second-stage transactions under framework agreements for goods listed in the centralized procurement catalogue under the Government Procurement Law; they may, per local circumstances, facilitate small-value off-catalogue goods purchases but purchasers cannot be forced to buy via the e-marketplace. Provincial finance departments must coordinate regional e-marketplace build-out, establish management systems, designate managers (centralized procurement agencies or other entrusted bodies), engage third-party providers for operation, maintenance and price monitoring, and implement supervision together with market regulators. E-marketplace managers are responsible for platform construction, operation and maintenance, drafting service agreements, improving product reference-price formation mechanisms and providing basic transaction support.

【兩部門：推進地方政府採購電子賣場高質量發展】財政部、市場監管總局釋出關於推進地方政府採購電子賣場高質量發展的指導意見。其中提出，明確電子賣場功能定位。電子賣場主要用於政府採購法規定的集中採購目錄以內貨物類產品框架協議採購第二階段的交易；可以根據實際情況，為目錄以外貨物類產品的小額零星採購提供服務，但不得強制要求採購人透過電子賣場採購。各省級財政部門要統籌推進本地區電子賣場建設，建立健全相關管理制度，明確集中採購機構、受委託的其他機構等電子賣場管理者，執行維護、價格監測等第三方服務機構，以及供應商等相關主體的權責，會同市場監管等部門實施監督管理。電子賣場管理者負責電子賣場建設與執行維護，制定電子賣場服務協議，完善電子賣場產品參考價形成機制，為電子賣場交易提供基礎保障。

Bulgaria Tourist Arrivals Fall 1.4% in April — Tourist arrivals in Bulgaria fell by 1.4% year-on-year to 786.5 thousand in April 2026, reversing a 0.5% increase in the previous month. This marked the first annual contraction in tourist arrivals since June 2025, as trips for other purposes declined by 4.8%, while professional visits fell by 1.3%, whereas holiday and recreation travel increased by 3.2%. EU citizens accounted for 55.4% of total arrivals, or 435.6 thousand, with the largest shares coming from Romania (41.2%) and Greece (30.8%). Meanwhile, visitors from other European countries made up 38.5%, or 303 thousand, led by Turkey at 60.2% of that group, or 182.5 thousand. In addition, transit passes accounted for 30.8% of all non-resident visits to Bulgaria, or 242.6 thousand.

Italy Jobless Rate Ticks Down to 5.1% in April — The unemployment rate in Italy edged down to 5.1% in April 2026 from 5.2% in March, below market expectations of 5.3%. This marked the lowest level on record, as the number of unemployed individuals fell by 18,000 month-on-month to 1.31 million, with declines recorded among both men and women and across all age groups except those aged 35–49. The employment rate rose 63.1% from 62.7% in each of the previous three months, with the number of employed persons rising by 123 thousand to 24.34 million. The inactivity rate decreased to 33.4% from 33.7%. Meanwhile, the youth unemployment rate, measuring jobseekers between 15 and 24 years old, fell back to a record low of 16.9% from 17.7% in March.

### 2026-05-29 16:16:44  #公司 #市場

【傑富瑞：拜耳關鍵藥品產品面臨競爭風險】Jefferies分析師表示，拜耳製藥業務勢頭良好，但中期內面臨風險，原因是其Kerendia腎病藥物和用於預防中風的asundexian等關鍵產品可能遭遇競爭。Jefferies稱，這家德國集團的製藥部門在2025至2030年期間的年度銷售額增幅應為2%，相比之下，同業平均增速為5%。這些分析師補充稱，在一項針對一種名為心房顫動的心律失常的試驗失敗後，asundexian的潛在市場已大幅縮小，而且百時美施貴寶的競爭藥物milvexian可能更具優勢。Jefferies表示，就拜耳的Kerendia而言，目前勢頭良好，但隨著阿斯利康和Mineralys Therapeutics正開發一類新藥，競爭威脅已在前方浮現。拜耳股價下跌1.3%。

### 2026-05-29 16:15:52  #MKT News #CSI300 #Impact bullish

【Report: China's government investment funds total ~6.56 tln yuan; hard tech now primary focus】A report says China had set up 1,513 government investment funds by end-2025 with combined scale of about 6.56 tln yuan. Beijing–Tianjin–Hebei ranks first by cumulative scale; the Yangtze River Delta has the most funds. New fund formation has slowed but average single-fund size rose to 8.65 bln yuan in 2025, up from about 2–4 bln before 2019. Government funds are increasingly active as LPs and are directing capital mainly into hard tech; reinvestment constraints have been relaxed, exit channels diversified, and due-diligence exemption and loss-tolerance mechanisms are being rolled out, easing prior reluctance to invest.

【US to set drone no-fly zones during 2026 World Cup】The FAA said on the 28th it will impose temporary flight restriction zones for the 2026 US-Canada-Mexico World Cup, coordinating with DHS and the DOJ. On match days all aircraft, including drones, will be banned within a 3-mile (≈4.8 km) radius of stadiums up to 3,000 ft (≈910 m) above ground level unless specially authorized. For fan events the no-fly zone will be a 1-mile (≈1.6 km) radius up to 1,000 ft (≈300 m). Hotels, team bases and training facilities will also face drone restrictions. Violations may carry fines up to $100,000, seizure of equipment and federal criminal charges.

### 2026-05-29 16:08:07  #Trading Economics #Markets #United Kingdom #Currency #Importance 1

Pound Slumps Over 1% in May — The pound was trading around $1.342 at the end of May, set for a monthly loss of over 1% against the USD. The decline came amid a mix of rising political uncertainty, following UK Prime Minister Keir Starmer’s Labour Party defeat in local elections, and ongoing US-Iran negotiations to end the three-month war, which has contributed to global inflation pressures. A tentative deal to extend the ceasefire by 60 days remains pending President Donald Trump’s approval. The UK’s economic challenges, including its lack of tech stocks, heavier reliance on oil, and overall pessimism about growth, have added to the currency’s struggles, as has its vulnerability to energy shocks. On the monetary policy front, investors have slightly scaled back expectations for further Bank of England rate hikes this year, as oil prices eased from four-year highs and recent UK data pointed to a cooling labor market, softer-than-expected inflation, and signs of moderating economic activity.

### 2026-05-29 16:02:22  #公司 #國際

【中國作協向海外推介推理懸疑、歷史玄幻與網路文學作家】由中國作家協會外聯部、中國作家協會網路文學中心、中國國際圖書貿易集團公司主辦的“推理懸疑作家國際推介會”和“歷史玄玄幻與網文作家國際推介會”日前在京舉行。推介會圍繞“推理敘事與人性迷局”“歷史想象與東方奇境”兩大主題展開，集中展示中國推理懸疑文學、歷史玄幻文學及網路文學的發展成果與青年創作力量。推介會上，來自美國、英國、法國等20多個國家的作家、出版人、翻譯家、學者及媒體代表對中國青年作家的創作風格和國際傳播價值開展交流。大家認為，中國的推理懸疑、歷史玄幻與網路文學等型別文學正在蓬勃發展，形成了蔚為大觀的文學景觀。這些作品既蘊含中華文化的獨特韻味，又具有極強的跨文化傳播能力，在海外吸引了包括“Z世代”青年人在內的一大批忠實讀者，為海外讀者進一步瞭解中國人的思想和情感世界提供了精彩的文學內容。不少海外出版人在聽完青年作家們的文學故事後，都表達了希望在本國引進或改編相關作品的興趣。據介紹，本次活動是中國作家協會“中國青年作家國際傳播計劃”的一部分，旨在進一步加強中國青年作家與國際出版界、文學界的交流合作，推動更多優秀中國文學作品走向世界，增進海外讀者對當代中國文學與文化的理解。（新華社）

【Toyota pauses development of next-generation Lexus EV】Toyota has paused development of the next-generation Lexus electric vehicle amid weak demand and the removal of US subsidies and is conducting a wider review of new projects. The LF‑ZC, originally targeted for a mid-2027 launch, was to include integrated die-casting and a lower-cost, faster-charging battery; Toyota said those technologies will be applied to other models. The move highlights automakers recalibrating electrification plans as EV demand growth slows and policy incentives become less certain. Toyota initially aimed for a 2026 production model; a spokesperson declined to comment on revised timing, though earlier reports said the schedule has been pushed to next year.

FTSE Edges Up, Set for Monthly Gain — The FTSE 100 edged slightly higher on Friday and remained on track for another monthly gain, rising 0.6% in May after a 2% increase in April, though the index is still around 4.4% below levels seen before the war began. Sentiment was supported by positive developments in the Middle East after reports that the US and Iran agreed to extend their ceasefire by 60 days, pending approval from President Donald Trump. Oil prices moved lower on expectations that the truce could hold. AstraZeneca gained around 1% after its blockbuster drug Imfinzi received US approval for the treatment of early bladder cancer. Defence stocks also advanced, with BAE Systems up 0.9% and Rolls Royce gaining 1.1%. Among banks, Lloyds and Barclays rose 0.7% and 1.2% respectively, while HSBC edged lower. Unilever also declined, while BAT dropped more than 1.5% and GSK fell over 0.5%.

### 2026-05-29 15:58:34  #Trading Economics #Economy #Thailand #Private Investment #Importance 0

Thailand Private Investment Weakens Further in April — Private investment in Thailand tumbled by 5.0% month-on-month in April 2026, deepening from a 3.5% decline in the previous month. It marked the steepest decline since December 2023, driven primarily by private vehicle and machinery and equipment index. The downturn was mainly driven by weaker investment in private vehicles and machinery and equipment. However, several components of investment activity remained relatively resilient with imports of machinery and equipment and domestic sales of machinery and equipment also posted gains. In addition, private construction investment rose during the month, supported by continued activity in both residential and infrastructure-related projects.

### 2026-05-29 15:51:58  #Trading Economics #Economy #Thailand #Retail Sales YoY #Importance 0

Thailand Retail Sales Growth Slows in March — Retail sales in Thailand rose 21.2% year-on-year in March 2026, easing from 31.4% in February, marking the softest growth in four months. The slowdown was mainly due to softer rises in other retail trade (36.7% vs 67% in February). Meanwhile, non-durable goods growth picked up to 12.5% (vs 4.7%), primarily supported by pharmaceutical and medical goods, cosmetics and toiletries (13.8% vs -1.0%). Durable goods rose 8.7% (vs 3.1%), but momentum was uneven as larger gains in computers and telecom equipment (18.9% vs 3.1%), audio and video equipment (14.3% vs 5.9%), textiles (38.3% vs 30.3%), and sporting goods (37.3% vs 27.4%) were partly offset by moderating growth in games and toys (29.5% vs 48.8%). Department stores and general stores rose 6.1% (vs 4.6%), while motor vehicles and automotive fuel rebounded 5.5% (vs -3.3%). On a monthly basis, retail sales grew 4.8%, recovering from a 25.3% drop in February.

### 2026-05-29 15:51:49  #Trading Economics #Economy #Thailand #Personal Spending #Importance 0

Thailand Personal Spending Falls Most Since 2021 — Private consumption in Thailand fell 2.1% month-on-month in April 2026, slipping further from a 0.8% decline in the previous month. It marked the steepest contraction since July 2021, mainly driven by weaker spending on non-durable goods, including consumer goods and fuel, amid higher energy costs that raised the cost of living, as well as adjustments in working and travel patterns amid significantly higher energy prices. The services index declined, weighed down by softer activity in hotels and restaurants, while expenditure by non-residents decreased. Meanwhile, consumer confidence decline amid energy prices linked to ongoing conflicts in the Middle East.

### 2026-05-29 15:49:35  #Trading Economics #Economy #Thailand #Current Account #Importance 0

Thailand Current Account Deficit Narrows — Thailand's current account surplus narrowed to USD 7.6 billion in April 2026 from USD 13.4 billion in the corresponding month a year earlier. The trade deficit widened sharply to USD 6.8 billion from USD 1.1 billion in April 2025, as imports (USD 37.7 billion) continued to outpace exports (USD 30.9 billion). Meanwhile, the deficit in services, primary income, and secondary income increased to USD 0.8 billion from USD 0.2 billion a year earlier. On the financial account, the overall surplus fell to USD 2.3 billion in April 2026 from USD 3.4 billion in the same period last year, largely due to other depository corporations recording a deficit of USD 2.3 billion, compared with a surplus of USD 1.1 billion a year earlier.

【Guangzhou’s 15th Five-Year Plan outline: expand financial platforms and back municipal financial holding firms to seek financial-holding license】Guangzhou’s 15th Five-Year Plan outline calls for enlarging and strengthening financial platforms and institutions. It instructs support for Guangzhou Futures Exchange to speed up listings of futures and options, back the introduction or establishment of futures intermediaries and risk‑management/service firms, attract firms tied to futures trading and asset allocation, and accelerate construction of a Nansha futures industrial park to promote spot‑futures linkage and a complete futures industry chain. The plan backs the Guangdong Equity Exchange to apply for business‑innovation pilot status and to raise the number of listed firms. It directs optimization of financial industry infrastructure, faster development of Guangzhou International Financial City to attract domestic and international financial institutions, research institutes and third‑party consultancies, and upgrading of the city’s private finance district. The city will support municipal financial holding companies in seeking financial‑holding licenses to expand asset scale and profitability, and encourage securities, fund and insurance firms to strengthen competitiveness and industry influence.

French Stocks Extend Monthly Gains — The CAC 40 rose 0.5% to 8,233 on Friday and was heading for a second consecutive monthly gain, supported by optimism over an extended ceasefire agreement between the US and Iran. Both countries had agreed to prolong the truce by 60 days to allow for formal negotiations and further discussions on Iran’s nuclear program, although US President Donald Trump has yet to formally approve the deal. On the economic front, France's economy contracted by 0.1% quarter-on-quarter in Q1 2026, reversing a 0.2% expansion in the previous quarter and marking the first contraction since Q2 2020. Preliminary data also showed annual inflation edged up to 2.4% in May from 2.2% in April, the highest since February 2024, driven largely by higher energy prices amid ongoing tensions in the Middle East. Financial stocks were among the top performers, including BNP Paribas (0.7%), Crédit Agricole (1.1%), and Société Générale (1.5%). Other gainers included LVMH (0.7%), Airbus (1.4%), and Stellantis (1.8%).

【WEF report: Middle East conflict, Strait of Hormuz shipping disruption raise global growth and inflation risks】The World Economic Forum’s Chief Economist Outlook says the Middle East conflict and shipping disruption in the Strait of Hormuz have increased the risk of a major global economic shock. Nearly 90% of surveyed chief economists expect global growth to slow over the next 12 months; as many as 94% expect global inflation to rise over the same period. Respondents judge the damage from a Strait of Hormuz shipping “blockage” to be materially larger than last year’s tariff disruptions. If shipping bottlenecks persist into H2, they would amplify supply-chain strains and push up energy and food costs. The report expects the Middle East and North Africa to be most affected with weak growth, sees sharply higher inflation expectations in Sub-Saharan Africa, and warns of a rising stagflation risk in Europe.

Poland Inflation Eases Slightly in May — The annual inflation rate in Poland edged down to 3.1% year-on-year in May 2026 from 3.2% in the previous month, according to a preliminary estimate. Despite the slight slowdown, price pressures remained elevated across key categories. Electricity, gas, and other fuels rose 5% from a year earlier, while fuel and lubricants for private transport equipment surged 12.3%, amid ongoing geopolitical tensions linked to the Middle East conflict. Meanwhile, food and non-alcoholic beverage prices increased at a more moderate pace of 0.5%. On a monthly basis, consumer prices fell by 0.3% in May, reversing a 0.6% increase in the preceding period.

China urged Japan and the Philippines to stop actions that infringe its maritime rights to preserve regional peace and stability.

【ECB says Iran war could lift euro-area consumers' medium-term inflation expectations】The ECB said in a blog a war involving Iran could push euro-area consumers' medium-term inflation expectations higher, strengthening the case for further rate hikes. It noted the 2022 Russia-Ukraine invasion and earlier geopolitical tensions left a "double hit" that has raised the probability households expect faster future price rises; policymakers are concerned energy-cost shocks could feed through into wages and corporate pricing. Short-term inflation expectations have risen markedly while medium-to-longer-term expectations have changed little. Markets largely price a 25bp ECB hike in two weeks; euro-area inflation is 3% and likely to pick up, and the ECB flagged the risk that short-term expectations may be extended into the medium term, creating upside risk to medium-term expectations.

India 10Y Edges Lower as Oil Retreats — The yield on India’s 10-Year G-Sec hovered near 6.9%, edging lower after the holiday break on easing oil prices and softer US Treasury yields. Bond sentiment was bolstered after reports suggested the US and Iran had agreed to extend their ceasefire arrangement, easing concerns over prolonged disruptions to global crude supply. Brent crude prices have fallen sharply this week, reducing inflationary pressure for oil-importing economies such as India. Meanwhile, US Treasury yields also declined, with the benchmark 10-year yield falling further after softer inflation data reinforced expectations that the Fed may avoid additional aggressive tightening. Still, gains in Indian bonds remained limited as investors stayed cautious ahead of New Delhi’s scheduled debt auction and the RBI’s policy decision next week. Market participants increasingly expect the RBI to keep interest rates unchanged, although some foreign institutions continue to anticipate the possibility of another rate hike.

Czech GDP Grows 0.2% in Q1 2026 — The Czech Republic’s gross domestic product grew by 0.2% quarter-on-quarter in the first quarter of 2026, easing from a 0.7% expansion in Q4 2025 and matching preliminary estimates, according to second estimates. The slowdown was primarily driven by domestic demand, with household consumption slowing to 0.6% from 1.2%, government spending declining 0.3% after stagnating, and gross fixed capital formation easing to 2% from 2.8%. Meanwhile, growth was supported by the trade, transportation, accommodation, and catering sector (+0.4%), finance and insurance (+4.3%), and professional, scientific, technical, and administrative activities (+0.9%). Construction (+0.2%) and real estate activities (+0.4%) also performed well.

Taiwan Stocks Mark 2nd Monthly Gain, End at Record High — The TAIEX, Taiwan’s benchmark stock index, climbed 1,097 points, or 2.5%, to close at 44,733 on Friday, erasing losses from the previous session, primarily supported by gains in electronic technology and technology services stocks. The broader index hit a new record high, surpassing the 44,000 level for the second time after closing at 44,257 on Wednesday, amid optimism over AI demand. A rally on Wall Street overnight also lifted sentiment, buoyed by the extended US-Iran ceasefire. Electronic technology stocks rose 2.7%, while technology services stocks surged 5.9%.
